Sits and talks with EBBA about what she sees: Bram is shouldering the burden -- literally -- rather than risk further injury or indignity to the boy. Could BEN get up on that step easily? He seems to be getting around well. If so: could Bram be got to use SINGLE-BUSHEL loads on the pretence that he's favouring the BOY? (Saves his pride.) Hauls out the LINIMENT (as for Aldy's hands); shows Ebba the RECIPE -- the miller's house is comfortable and could make more, even keep some to spare. On slate or paper: scratches out a PULLEY SYSTEM, like Sabeth's laundry hoist.
- (no roll) EBBA's means: TEA -- nettle and mint, honeyed @@*; a SLATE and a stub of chalk from the tally-shelf (the mill keeps its grain tallies on it -- wiped). Gear = permission (ordinary household kit).
- (no roll; plain look already had, S15 l.335 + today) BENNO on the hopper-step: YES -- a low block, one step up, standing still, both hands on the sack's mouth; no load on the leg beyond his own weight. Fine.

Then, on the recipe: watches the liniment made in her own kitchen -- lard warmed with a little beeswax, crushed rosemary, a pinch of ground mustard (Cari's recipe, l.2888) -- and repeats each one back. - RULING: the PULLEY SKETCH = TINKER EL6 (mechanisms), +1 free Boost (Sabeth's laundry hoist, seen and used -- a pre-existing reference; capped by Tinker rank 1) = 7d vs the job's COMPLEXITY 2 @@* (one beam, one rope, a block, a hook; the hopper's lip and the ladder in the way). An ACTION (ties favour the defender -- S15 clarification): EFFECT 0 = the idea only; Garrow will have to work it out himself (no Boost); EFFECT 1-2 = WORKABLE as drawn -> its effect as a BOOST to the building; EFFECT 3+ = SOUND -- includes a BRACE for the old beam and a PAWL to hold a load mid-lift.
[Cari Tinker: pulley sketch on the slate (EL6+1 vs 2)] 7d: 1 0 0 0 1 0 1 = 3
- RESULT: 3 vs 2 -> EFFECT 1: WORKABLE as drawn -- a rope over the beam, a block at the beam, a sack-hook, the free end belayed to a cleat on the post; a +1 BOOST to whoever builds it (GARROW for the block and hook; a carpenter @@ for the cleat). - GEAR: the SLATE sketch (Ebba's tally slate -- she won't wipe it).

DEDICATION (a turnip to each bowl): "For the watchful sky above; / For the bountiful earth below; / For the seas which give us rain; / For the fires which give us warmth; / And for the crafts which teach us to make of these the dutiful and prosperous lives we seek." (Celestial opening: "Hear me, ye gods of Heaven who watch over us all -- / The good citizens of this town...") -> QUOTE SHEET (wrap): Cari's DUSK OFFICE at Crossroads [Lee]. -- the invocation; then a TURNIP placed in each bowl with solemnity at each line of the dedication (sky, earth, seas, fires, crafts). Steps back; bows. Then: "Done, but now, let's do it again, and you tell me which is better." The SAME prayer in colloquial, conversational COMMON, the turnips placed again. Then gestures for ROOK to light the taper.

  - SHE IS ILSE TARRO, wife of OLEF TARRO (grain-factor of Ganth), WENNA's MOTHER. Forties; a counting-house wife -- lettered, convent-schooled as a girl (hence the Celestial forms); good cloak, plain on purpose.
  - The WHITE LOCK: WENNA described the Sister who opened her door. ("Good" = the right one.)
  - THE EARLIER SURETY: her first child, a son, PIRAN TARRO, taken as surety SIX years ago at nine, against a bad-harvest debt. They were told, after the debt was paid, that the boy had "asked to stay" and taken a novice's vows on the Mountain; letters came, twice, in a clerk's hand, then none. She has NOT been told he is dead. She does not know what "hallowed" means. (TRUTH, GM-ONLY: four marks, "hallowed" -- Piran is DEAD, under Calder's work, like the Corvane child. Where his remains lie and whether he was "walked": UNDECIDED -- don't harden.)
  - WHY she came three times and "wouldn't say": fear of the Order (they had both her children); she came to Crossroads first (S13) after Wenna came home, to find the Sister and ask after Piran, and lost her nerve twice. Since the Convocation's news ran down the Ganth road ("the Hierarch in a cart"), she came back.
  - WHAT CARI ALREADY KNOWS (WKW): the Tarro HOUSEHOLD line in Calder's book: four marks, "HALLOWED" -- an EARLIER Tarro surety; and Kade's sworn word: "That's what hallowed is" (the Corvane child, dead and walked). Cari does NOT know the earlier child's name (it's in Tobin's tally; the Visitor read the surety lines).

  - "Olef cleared it the second autumn. Every copper. And they wrote that Piran had ASKED to stay. That he'd taken a novice's vows. That he was well and at his prayers." A breath. "Two letters. A clerk's hand -- not his. Piran couldn't spell 'well.'" Something that was once a laugh. "Then nothing."

- @@* (situation, no roll): the cottage lit and warm; the new patch of thatch dark against the stars, tight; a wood fire built up proper in the hearth, the eel-pot on its hook, simmering. The pallets made up, the floor swept, the onion-stirring spoon on the shelf. Nobody but HARROW, on the settle by the fire in Rook's da's coat, a pipe unlit in his teeth, minding the pot and looking very pleased with himself.
- @@* HARROW (from character -- laconic; a knighthood is "it'll keep the rain off"): takes the pipe out. "Gone." A jerk of the head toward the square. "Councilman's. All of 'em." A pause to let it land. "Liss in a borrowed shawl. Aldy's, I'd say -- the blue one." Another pause. "Tom in a clean shirt and a face like he's going to his hanging. Moll carried the onion." (Of course she did.) "Linnet yelled the whole way." He puts the pipe back. Round it: "Wat come to fetch 'em. Didn't know what to do with his hands." Then the nearest Harrow comes to a speech: "I'm minding the pot. Eels. They'll want 'em when they're back, an' they'll not have eaten, for the nerves."
- WKW: CARI -- LISS and the children at the PRYCES' for supper tonight (Wat fetched them); Harrow minding the Fenn hearth and the eel-pot.
- NO ROLL.
- [Lee] Grins; leaves HARROW to it. Back to TESSA's. Eases in quietly; BUILDS UP THE FIRE; takes off her SHOES; covers her up.
- GM TRUTH (set before the roll; from l.~3606: Tessa WAKES at every sound near the HEARTH -- she lies listening for Robbie and the kettle): the fire-building is exactly the sound she wakes to. RULING: Cari's quiet care = HEALER EL9 (bedside hands; the shoes, the blanket, the fire laid gently) vs TESSA's WATCHFULNESS, Level 3 (3d). An ACTION (ties favour the defender): WIN = she sleeps through all of it; TIE (effect 0) = she stirs at the hearth, half-wakes -- "Rob?" -- and a word sends her back under; LOSS = she wakes with a start, reaching for the empty cot, frightened. (Either of the last two is the sleep talk's opening; neither is harm.)
[Cari Healer: quiet care at Tessa's (EL9)] 9d: 1 1 1 1 1 0 1 1 1 = 8
[  Tessa's watchfulness (Lv3)] 3d: 1 1 0 = 2
- RESULT: 8 vs 2 -> WIN, effect 6: TESSA SLEEPS THROUGH ALL OF IT.
- @@* (situation): the fire laid from the stack by the door (muster wood, split small), built up slow so it takes without a crack; the shoes eased off -- a darned heel, a sole worn thin; the shawl untangled; a blanket from the chest and Corin's heavy drover's cloak off the peg over that. Tessa's hanging arm lifted back onto the bed; her hand finds the cloak's edge in her sleep and holds it. Her breathing goes long and slow. The room warms. Nothing in her listens.
- WKW: none (Tessa asleep).
- [Lee OOC] "And I didn't even get to seduce-tease her, lol" (the joke saved for the morning, perhaps).
- [Lee] Sheds down to her SHIFT; climbs into CORIN's side of the bed; SLEEPS.
